def next_pow2(x): return 1 << (int(x).bit_length()-1?)

```
def conv_max_normalized(a):
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,None)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf, np.nan, 1.0
    a = a/s
    n = a.size
    L = 1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f*f, n=L)[:2*n-1]
    # numerical negatives
    cmax = float(c.max())
    return 2.0*n*cmax, cmax, c
```
Need use same L for gradient to avoid recalculating? Could compute in helper.

```
def cross_weights_a(w,a,L=None):
   n=a.size
   if L is None: L=1; while L<3*n-2: L <<=1
   ar = a[::-1]
   wpad = np.zeros(2*n-1); wpad[:] = w
   F = np.fft.rfft(ar, n=L) * np.fft.rfft(wpad, n=L)
   conv = np.fft.irfft(F, n=L)[:3*n-2]
   s = conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n
   return s
```
But L for c length was for 2n-1; cross needs 3n-2. Could just compute separately; cost okay. Or derive cross using w length 2n-1 and a via direct if n small? For n=2048, O(n^2) too heavy. Use FFT.
Then g = a + s.

Param optimization with q basis:
```
def param_density(q, n):
   x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   qvals = q[0]*(x-0.5) + sum(q[1...]*cos, sin)
   z = qvals - max(qvals)
   e = np.exp(z)
   a = e/e.sum()
   return a
```
Basis count D. We can precompute basis matrix B shape (n,D) for each n,K. But to save memory, D<=50, n=8192 => 400k floats ok. Build in top-level? Need no closures; pass n,K,seed.
Gradient wrt q: qvals_j not? If B precomputed, grad_q = B.T @ grad_a_density? Actually grad_a_i is derivative wrt a_i. grad_q = B.T @ (a_i*(g_a_i - dot(a,g_a))). For linear maybe. Good.

Param optimizer may be faster than direct. Let's design:
```
def param_start_to_quality(q0,n,K,beta,iterations,lr,seed):
    build B
    q=q0 copy; m=zeros; v=zeros; best=inf; best_a=None
    for it in range(iterations):
       q = clip(q,-20,20)
       z = B @ q
       z -= max
       e=exp(z); a=e/sum
       score,c = fast_score(a) maybe c
       if finite and score<best: update
       weights softmax beta c
       g_a = a + cross(w,a)
       g_a /= beta? for gradient F? For Adam, scale constant; can set g_a /= beta to avoid large lr? Actually beta large -> g in [~1/n], βc weights, F gradient =g_no/beta. Use g = (a+s)/beta.
       mean=np.dot(a,g)
       grad = B.T @ (a*(g-mean))
       adam step
       lr decay
    return best, best_a
```

def fft_conv_full(a):

```
def weighted_gradient_a(a, w, Lc=None):
 n=a.size; L=1; while L<3*n-2: L<<=1
 ar=a[::-1]
 F=np.fft.rfft(ar,n=L); W=np.fft.rfft(w,n=L) # w length 2n-1, rest zeros
 conv=np.fft.irfft(F*W,n=L)[:3*n-2]
 return a + conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
But w sum maybe not exactly1; if not, formula includes ∑w a_i. We can normalize w sum1. If w sum=1, g = a + s. If w sum other, g=(sumw)a+s; we can normalize.

Param Adam:
```
def adam_update(q, m, v, grad, t, lr, beta1, beta2, eps):
 m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
 v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
 mh = m/(1-beta1**t); vh=v/(1-beta2**t)
 q = q - lr*mh/np.sqrt(vh+eps)
 clip q
 return q,m,v
```
Normalize grad before update to lr 0.005-0.02? If normalized to norm1, lr maybe 0.01.

   B[:,idx]=np.sin(...); idx+=1

We can use this algorithm as a search method. Parameters: n, topK weight update, CG iterations, damping.
Pseudo:
```
def reweighted_kkt(a0, n, iters, topK=32, cg_iter=50, seed):
   a=normalize(a0)
   for it:
      w = make_weights_topk(c_of_a(a), n, topK)  # maybe softmax blend
      # solve A a = 1
      A = I + M_w. Use CG starting x=a (scaled maybe RHS sum?)
      rhs = np.ones(n)
      x = a copy
      for cg in range(cg_iter):
         r = rhs - (x + M_w x)
         p = r (or previous)
         Ap = p + M_w p
         alpha = r.dot(r)/p.dot(Ap)
         x += alpha p
         r_new = r - alpha Ap
         if norm small break
         if beta... p = r_new + beta p
      x = clip positive, normalize
      # optional convex mix with old a to avoid divergence: a_new = (1-t)a + t x
      a = normalize( (1-t) a + t x )
      update best
```
But M_w matvec cost FFT each CG iteration. 50 CG *2 matvec? Actually Matvec x and p, can combine? Need 2 FFT per CG. For n=2048, 50*4 FFT ~200 FFT per outer, too heavy if 100 outer (20k FFT) maybe seconds-to-minutes. But matvec can be optimized by precomputing FFT of w for each outer, then each matvec is one rfft of vector plus inverse? Let's implement `hankel_matvec_w_a(w, a, W_fft, L)`:
We have formula s_i = conv(ar, w)[n-1+i]. If W_fft fixed for w, ar=a[::-1]. Matvec = irfft(rfft(ar,L)*W_fft). Need one rfft + one irfft per matvec. CG needs Ap = p + matvec(p). So one rfft+irfft per iteration. 50 outer =>100 FFT transforms. For n=2048, L=8192, each transform ~0.1ms? 100*0.1=0.01s per outer? Actually 100 FFTs maybe 0.1s. 100 outer 10s. Good.
Could reduce CG iterations 30.

Can we diagonalize A using FFT if w has special form? General Hankel not circulant. CG good. Need A SPD: I+M with M PSD? M symmetric but not necessarily PSD? Hankel with nonnegative w may be PSD? It's Gram? (M a)_i = ∑_j w_{i+j} a_j. For a, a^T M a = ∑_{i,j} a_i a_j w_{i+j} = ∑_k w_k (∑_{i+j=k}a_i a_j) = w^T c ≥0. Yes M is PSD! Great. A SPD. CG converges. Precondition? Identity. Condition maybe large but okay. We can stop at residual tolerance.

After solve, we should ensure c not too high. If solution creates peaks, update weights. This is like reweighted Tikhonov.

def softmax_from_c(c, beta=None, topK=None, smooth=0.05):
   c = np.maximum(c,0)
   cmax=float(c.max()); cmin? 
   if topK is not None:
       k = min(len(c), max(1, int(topK)))
       idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
       w = np.zeros_like(c); w[idx]=1/k; return w
   # adaptive beta: scale = cmax - np.median(c) or cmax*0.1
   scale = max(0.05*cmax, 1e-12)
   z=(c-cmax)/scale
   w=np.exp(z)
   w/=w.sum()
   return w
```

Let's design `kkt_search(a, time_limit, topK=None, soft_width=0.1, mix=0.2, cg_iters=40, damp_initial=0.5, seed=123, label='')`.
Pseudo:
```
def kkt_search(a, time_limit=30, soft_width=0.1, mix_top=0.3, topK=40, cg_iters=40, damp=0.5, label=''):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a copy)
    n=a.size
    best=a; best_score=score(a)
    it=0
    while time < deadline:
        c, Lc = fft_conv(a)
        score = 2*n*max(c)
        if score<best: update
        w = make_weights(c, n, topK, soft_width, mix_top)
        # ensure w positive maybe add 1e-6 uniform
        w += 1e-9
        w/=w.sum()
        Lx=1; while Lx<3*n-2: Lx<<=1
        W_fft = np.fft.rfft(w, n=Lx)
        x = a * n  # start
        # CG
        b = np.ones(n)
        r = b - (x + cross(x,W_fft))
        p = r.copy()
        rs = r.dot(r)
        for cg in range(cg_iters):
            Ap = p + cross(p,W_fft)
            pAp = p.dot(Ap)
            if pAp <=0: break
            alpha = rs/pAp
            x += alpha*p
            r -= alpha*Ap
            rs_new = r.dot(r)
            if rs_new < tol*rs or sqrt(rs_new) < 1e-8: break
            beta_cg = rs_new/rs
            p = r + beta_cg*p
            rs=rs_new
        x = clip positive; sum
        if sum<=0: x=a
        else x/=sum
        # maybe damp
        if it < 5: mixf=0.5
        else mixf=min(1.0, damp + 0.1*??)
        a_new = (1-mixf)*a + mixf*x
        a_new = normalize_nonneg(a_new)
        # To prevent stagnation, occasionally add random floor/logit
        if it % 50 == 0 and rng.random<0.3: a_new = normalize(0.98*a_new + 0.02*random_positive)
        a=a_new
        it++
    return best,best_score
```
But `cross` in CG needs W_fft and Lx. Define helper `hankel_cross_with_fft(a, W_fft, Lx)` where W_fft is rfft of w length Lx. Need also n for slice; compute n-len array. We can derive n from len(a). Implement:
```
def hankel_cross(a, W_fft, Lx):
    n=a.size
    ar=a[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,n=Lx)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*W_fft,n=Lx)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
This uses W_fft precomputed for w padded length Lx. Good.
`c, Lc` function can also return c and maybe cmax. Use same L? For c, Lc enough 2n-1; can be different from Lx for cross. For w, length 2n-1; W_fft length Lx.

def normalize_nonneg_vec(x):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
   x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
   s=x.sum()
   if s<=0: x=np.ones(x.size)/x.size; return x
   return x/s
```

def next_pow2_ge(m):
   if m<=1: return 1

Direct top-K gradient (`gradient_refine`):
```
def gradient_refine(a, time_limit, lr=0.3, topK=32, soft_frac=0.1, top_mix=0.5, seed):
 a=normalize; best...
 while time:
   c=fft_conv; score; update
   w=make_weights
   g = a + hankel_cross(a, W_fft, Lx)
   # normalize gradient: shift by median and scale by rms
   g -= np.median(g)
   scale = np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g))
   if scale>0: g/=scale
   # multiplicative
   a = a * np.exp(-lr*g)
   a=clip floor normalize
   # optional adaptive lr decay
   lr *= 0.999
 return best
```
This is essentially first-order on top weights. It may refine KKT.

Param Adam details:
```
def param_search(n, K, iterations, q0, beta_weights? use topK weights, cg? No, use gradient from logsumexp or top weights.
 Build B.

 for it:
   q=clip(-15,15)

   score,c=fft_score(a); update best (store q,a)

   # gradient of smooth F? Use g_no = a + cross(w,a)
   # If using top weights non-smooth, treat subgradient.
   g_no = a + hankel_cross(a, W_fft, Lx)
   # Normalize to softmax gradient
   # Since a is softmax q, dF/dq = a*(g - sum(a*g))
   grad = B.T @ (a * (g_no - np.dot(a,g_no)))
   # Normalize gradient scale
   gscale = norm(grad)
   if gscale>0: grad /= gscale
   # Adam lr 0.005 maybe
```

### Helpers

```
def _pow2_ge(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        x = np.ones_like(x)
        return x / x.size
    x /= s
    # ensure finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       x = np.ones_like(x); return x/x.size
    return x
```

`fft_convolve_full`:
```
def fft_convolve_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = _pow2_ge(2*n - 1)
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f*f, n=L)[:2*n-1]
    # Clean tiny negatives
    c = np.where((c < 0.0) & (c > -1e-12), 0.0, c)
    return c, L
```
Could set all c<0 to0? If FFT numerical negative larger? Nonnegative true, but if rounding negative -1e-16. Use `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`; but if real negative due instability for large values? Values normalized small; okay. Setting negative to0 may slightly understate sum, but max nonneg. Use maximum.

`fft_score`:
```
def fft_score(a):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   c,L=fft_convolve_full(a)
   cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else np.inf
   if not np.isfinite(cmax): return np.inf, c
   return float(2.0*a.size*cmax), c
```

`hankel_cross_with_fft`:
```
def hankel_cross_with_fft(a, W_fft, Lx):
    n=a.size
    ar=a[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(ar, n=Lx)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*W_fft, n=Lx)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need Lx >= 3n-2. Ensure before.

`make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(c, n, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix):
   c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
   c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
   w_soft = None
   cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   cmean = float(np.mean(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   scale = max(soft_frac * (cmax - cmean), 1e-12)
   z = (c - cmax) / scale
   w_soft = np.exp(z)
   ssoft = w_soft.sum()
   if ssoft > 0: w_soft /= ssoft
   else: w_soft = np.ones(c.size)/c.size
   if top_k <= 0:
       top_w = w_soft
   else:
       k = int(min(c.size, top_k))
       if k < c.size:
          idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
       else:
          idx = np.arange(c.size)
       top_w = np.zeros(c.size)
       top_w[idx] = 1.0/k
   if top_mix <= 0:
       w = w_soft
   elif top_mix >= 1:
       w = top_w
   else:
       w = (1-top_mix)*w_soft + top_mix*top_w
   w = w + 1e-12
   w /= w.sum()
   return w
```
If cmax=0, scale 1e-12, z=0, soft uniform.

`cg_solve_a`:
```
def cg_solve_a(w, a_guess, cg_iters, tol):
    n = a_guess.size
    Lx = _pow2_ge(3*n - 2)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=Lx)
    b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # Start scaled so sum approx n
    s0 = a_guess.sum()
    if s0 > 0:
        x = a_guess * (n / s0)
    else:
        x = np.ones(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.ones(n)
    r = b - (x + hankel_cross_with_fft(x, W, Lx))
    p = r.copy()
    rs = float(r.dot(r))
    if rs < 1e-30:
        x = x / x.sum() if x.sum()>0 else x
        return x
    for _ in range(cg_iters):
        Ap = p + hankel_cross_with_fft(p, W, Lx)
        pAp = float(p.dot(Ap))
        if pAp <= 1e-14:
            break
        alpha = rs / pAp
        x = x + alpha * p
        r = r - alpha * Ap
        rs_new = float(r.dot(r))
        if not np.isfinite(rs_new):
            break
        if np.sqrt(rs_new) < tol:
            break
        beta = rs_new / rs
        p = r + beta * p
        rs = rs_new
    # clean
    x = np.real(x)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e6)
    s = x.sum()
    if s <= 0: return None
    return x
```
Need r type; operations with FFT real okay.

Potential overflow if x large. Damping later. CG from x ~1, A entries? okay.

`kkt_search`:
```
def kkt_search(a, time_limit, top_k_ratio=0.02, soft_frac=0.1, top_mix=0.3, cg_iters=35, seed=1):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    top_k = max(8, min(128, int(n*top_k_ratio)))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + time_limit
    best = a.copy(); best_score = np.inf
    it=0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + it)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       score,c = fft_score(a)
       if score < best_score:
           best = a.copy(); best_score=score; last_improve=it
       if best_score <= 1.0? break
       w = make_weights(c, n, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix)
       x = cg_solve_a(w, a, cg_iters, tol=1e-7)
       if x is None:
           # fallback small gradient
           a = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,a.size)))
           it +=1; continue
       # damp factor
       if it < 10: mixf = 0.5
       elif it < 50: mixf = 0.8
       else: mixf = 1.0
       a_new = (1-mixf)*a + mixf*x
       # occasional random positivity floor
       if it % 100 == 0:
           eps = 1e-6
           a_new = (1-eps)*a_new + eps*np.ones_like(a_new)
       a = normalize_nonneg(a_new)
       # Check finite
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): break
       it += 1
    # final maybe exact score? not here
    return best, best_score
```
Increment it? In loop start with it=0; after update it+=1. best update uses current a. Good.
But `it` used in random seed; okay. Top_k maybe fixed. Could adapt top_k decreasing with it: `top_k_cur = max(4, int(top_k * max(0.2, 1 - it/200)))`. But keep simple.

Potential time: CG uses W_fft computed each outer; no reuse.

`gradient_refine`:
```
def gradient_refine(a, time_limit, lr=0.25, top_k=32, soft_frac=0.1, top_mix=0.4, cg? no, seed=0):
   a=normalize...
   deadline...
   best...
   scale_decay...
   for it:
       score,c
       update
       w=make_weights(c,n,...)
       Lx=_pow2_ge(3*n-2); W=np.fft.rfft(w,n=Lx)
       g = a + hankel_cross_with_fft(a,W,Lx)
       # shift/scale
       g -= np.median(g)
       mscale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)))
       if mscale <=0: break
       g /= mscale
       # lr schedule
       step = lr * np.exp(-it/2000)
       a = a * np.exp(-step*g)
       a = clip floor normalize
       it++
   return best,best_score
```

Normalizing g by rms; if g has positive and negative, exp can increase some, decrease others. But sum normalization means adding constant to g irrelevant. Shift by median okay. We should ensure `step` not too large; 0.25 maybe changes up to e^0.25 ~1.28. Could be okay. If score worsens, best kept. For many iterations, may wander. Good.

Param search implementation:
```
def make_basis(n, D): # Chebyshev T_1..T_D
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n; t=2*x-1
   B=np.empty((n,D))
   # T0 = ones
   prev=ones; curr=t.copy() # T1
   for k in range(1,D+1):
      if k==1: B[:,k-1]=T1
      elif k==2: B[:,k-1]=T2 = 2*t^2 -1
      else: T_k = 2*t*T_{k-1} - T_{k-2}; B[:,k-1]=T_k
   return B
```

`param_search(n,D,iterations,q0,params)`:
```
def param_search(n, D, time_limit, q0, seed=1, topK=40, soft_frac=0.1, top_mix=0.3, lr=0.01):
   B=make_basis(n,D)
   q=q0.copy()
   m=np.zeros(D); v=np.zeros(D); best...; best_q=q.copy()
   t=0
   deadline...
   while ...:
      q = np.clip(q,-20,20)
      z = B.dot(q)
      z -= z.max()
      e = np.exp(z)
      a=e/e.sum()
      score,c=fft_score(a)
      if score<best: update best_q= q.copy()
      # weights
      w=make_weights(c,...)
      Lx=...; W=...
      g = a + hankel_cross_with_fft(a,W,Lx)
      # softmax gradient
      dot = float(np.dot(a,g))
      grad_a = a*(g-dot)
      grad = B.T.dot(grad_a)
      grad_scale = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
      if grad_scale > 1e-12: grad /= grad_scale
      t +=1
      # Adam
      b1=0.9;b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
      m = b1*m+(1-b1)*grad
      v = b2*v+(1-b2)*grad*grad
      mh=m/(1-b1**t); vh=v/(1-b2**t)
      q = q - lr*mh/np.sqrt(vh+eps)
      # occasional projection? q clip
   # reconstruct from best_q
   z=B.dot(best_q); a=exp...
   return best_a,best_score,best_q
```

`make_weights` detailed:
```
def make_weights(c, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop):
   cmax,...
   if use_prop:
       eps = max(1e-9, 1e-6*max(1.0, cmean))
       base = np.maximum(c,0.0) + eps
       w_soft = base/base.sum()
   else:
       soft as exp
   top_w uniform or topk
   if top_mix<=0: w=w_soft
   elif top_mix>=1: w=top_w
   else: w=(1-top_mix)*w_soft + top_mix*top_w

def upsample_sequence(a, n_new):
    xold=(np.arange(len(a))+0.5)/len(a)
    xnew=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
    return np.interp(xnew, xold, a)

def choose_best_exact(pool, best_arr, best_score, deadline):
   candidates = []
   if best_arr is not None: candidates.append(best_arr)
   for sc, arr in pool[-4:]: candidates.append(arr)
   # add unique by object identity? not crucial
   best_exact_score = np.inf; best_exact_arr = best_arr
   for arr in candidates[-6:]:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
       try: sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
       except Exception: sc = np.inf
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
           best_exact_score=sc; best_exact_arr=arr
   return best_exact_arr, best_exact_score
```

#### make_weights details:
```
def make_weights(c, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    cmean = float(np.mean(c))
    # soft
    if use_prop:
        eps = max(1e-9, 1e-6 * max(1.0, cmean))
        w_soft = c + eps
        s = float(w_soft.sum())
        if s > 0: w_soft /= s
        else: w_soft = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
    else:
        scale = max(soft_frac * max(1e-12, cmax - cmean), 1e-12)
        z = (c - cmax) / scale
        w_soft = np.exp(z)
        s = float(w_soft.sum())
        if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s): w_soft /= s
        else: w_soft = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
    # top_w
    if top_k <= 0:
        top_w = w_soft.copy()
    else:
        k = int(min(c.size, max(1, top_k)))
        if k >= c.size:
            top_w = np.ones(c.size) / c.size
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            top_w = np.zeros(c.size)
            top_w[idx] = 1.0 / k
    if top_mix <= 0:
        w = w_soft
    elif top_mix >= 1:
        w = top_w
    else:
        w = (1.0 - float(top_mix)) * w_soft + float(top_mix) * top_w
    w = w + 1e-12
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s > 0: w /= s
    else: w = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
    return w
```
`soft_frac` if 0? scale 1e-12 top-like. okay.

`cg_solve_a`: Need if `cg_iters` maybe 0. If no CG, return guess. Use `tol=1e-6`.
Potential memory: b ones, x, r, p. okay.
```
def cg_solve_a(w, a_guess, cg_iters=30, tol=1e-6):
    n=a_guess.size
    if n ==0: return None
    Lx = pow2_ge(3*n -2 if n>1 else 1)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=Lx)
    b = np.ones(n)
    s_guess = float(a_guess.sum())
    if s_guess > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(a_guess)):
        x = a_guess * (n / s_guess)
    else:
        x = np.ones(n)
    r = b - (x + hankel_cross_with_fft(x, W, Lx))
    p = r.copy()
    rs = float(r.dot(r))
    for _ in range(int(cg_iters)):
       Ap = p + hankel_cross_with_fft(p, W, Lx)
       pAp = float(p.dot(Ap))
       if pAp <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(pAp): break
       alpha = rs / pAp
       x = x + alpha * p
       r = r - alpha * Ap
       rs_new = float(r.dot(r))
       if not np.isfinite(rs_new): break
       if np.sqrt(rs_new) < max(tol, 1e-10): break
       beta = rs_new / rs if rs > 0 else 0.0
       p = r + beta * p
       rs = rs_new
    x = np.real(x)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return None
    x = np.clip(x,0,1e6)
    sx=x.sum()
    if sx <=0: return None
    x = x/sx
    return x
```
CG starting x sum n. If a_guess nonfinite, ones.
Potential if x huge, `r` finite? okay.

`fft_convolve_full`: `L = pow2_ge(2*n -1)`.
If n=0? But sequences nonempty. For n=1, 2n-1=1 pow2=1. `f*f` real, irfft length1. Good.

`fast_score`: If c all negative due FFT? `cmax=max(c)` after maximum? We use maximum so cmax≥0. Score 0 possible only zero sequence; but normalized sum1, cmax>0.
```
def fft_score(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    c, L = fft_convolve_full(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0*a.size*cmax), c
```

`hankel_cross_with_fft`: ensure Lx enough; if W_fft length maybe from rfft of w padded to Lx, okay. If Lx smaller? Assured.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n_old=a.size
    if n_old == n_new: return a.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)/n_old
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    if n_new == 1: ...
    return normalize_nonneg(y)
```
If a has zeros, interp linear okay.

`make_basis` as above.
`sequence_from_basis`:
```
def sequence_from_basis(q, B):
    z = B.dot(q)
    z -= np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(B.shape[0])/B.shape[0]
    return e/s
```
If B is empty? D>0.

`param_search`: Use D basis. Need if D maybe 0. Return best.
```
def param_search(n, D, time_limit, q0, settings=None, lr=0.006, seed=1):
    if settings is None: settings=[...]
    B = make_basis(n,D)
    q = np.asarray(q0,float).copy()
    if q.size != D: q = np.resize(q,D)
    m=np.zeros(D); v=np.zeros(D)
    best_q=q.copy(); best_a=sequence_from_basis(best_q,B); best_score=fft_score(best_a)[0]
    t=0; it=0
    deadline = time.monotonic()+time_limit
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        a=sequence_from_basis(q,B)
        score,c=fft_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score<best_score:
            best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); best_q=q.copy()
        if best_score <=1.01: break
        set=settings[it%len(settings)]; ...
        w=make_weights(...)
        Lx=...; W=...
        g = a + hankel_cross_with_fft(a,W,Lx)
        dot = float(np.dot(a,g))
        grad_a = a*(g-dot)
        grad = B.T.dot(grad_a)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm>0 and np.isfinite(gnorm): grad = grad/gnorm
        else: break
        t+=1
        b1=0.9; b2=0.999
        m = b1*m+(1-b1)*grad
        v = b2*v+(1-b2)*grad*grad
        mh=m/(1-b1**t)
        vh=v/(1-b2**t)
        q = q - lr*mh/np.sqrt(vh+1e-8)
        q = np.clip(q,-20,20)
        it+=1
    return best_a, best_score, best_q
```
`b1**t` for t large okay. t can be large; use `1 - b1**t` maybe underflow to 0? For t>1000, b1**t small, denom1. fine.
If D=0, make_basis error; ensure D>=1.

`make_start_collection`: Use list. Need ensure arrays positive. For x^p, x min 0.5/n >0. Good.
```
def make_start_collection(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.ones(n))
    starts.append(x.copy())
    starts.append((1.0-x).copy())
    for p in [0.5,2.0]:
       starts.append(np.power(x,p)); starts.append(np.power(1.0-x,p))
    for s in [1.0,3.0,6.0,12.0]:
       starts.append(np.exp(s*(x-0.5))); starts.append(np.exp(-s*(x-0.5)))
    for mu,sg in [(0.25,0.08),(0.25,0.2),(0.75,0.08),(0.75,0.2),(0.5,0.15),(0.5,0.35)]:
       starts.append(gaussian_start(n,mu,sg))
    # mix two gauss
    g1=gaussian_start(n,0.3,0.12); g2=gaussian_start(n,0.7,0.12)
    starts.append(0.4*g1+0.6*g2)
    starts.append(0.6*g1+0.4*g2)
    # random trig
    for k in range(3):
       M=4
       amp=rng.uniform(0.3,1.5)
       q = amp*rng.normal(0,1,M)
       ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,M)
       vals = np.zeros(n)
       for m in range(M):
           vals += q[m]*np.cos(2*np.pi*(m+1)*(x-0.5)+ph[m])
       starts.append(np.exp(vals))
    # random positive
    starts.append(rng.uniform(0.1,1.0,n))
    out=[]
    for s in starts:
       a=np.asarray(s,float)
       if a.size==n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
          a=np.clip(a,0,None)
          if a.sum()>0:
             a=a/a.sum()
             out.append(a)
    return out
```
No nested. `gaussian_start` uses x inside.

`gaussian_start`:
```
def gaussian_start(n,mu,sigma):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    return np.exp(-0.5*((x-mu)/max(sigma,1e-6))**2)
```

def record_candidate(pool, arr, score, max_pool=10):
    if arr is None or not np.isfinite(score): return pool
    if len(pool) >= max_pool: pool = pool[1:]
    pool.append(arr.copy())
    return pool

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2).

The code combines several complementary heuristics:
1. A reweighted KKT fixed-point method:
   for weights w on convolution coefficients, solve
       (I + H_w) a = 1
   where (H_w a)_i = sum_j w_{i+j} a_j.  This is solved by conjugate
   gradients using FFT matrix-vector products.  The weights are updated from
   the current convolution profile (top-k / soft / proportional modes).
2. Multiplicative first-order refinement using the same gradient idea.
3. Chebyshev-parameterized smooth densities optimized with Adam-like updates.
4. Random block perturbations as a final fallback/refinement.

All helpers are top-level and use no closures or lambdas.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def pow2_ge(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(x):
    """Clip to [0, 1000] and normalize to sum 1."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x.ndim != 1:
        x = x.ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        x = np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.size)
    x /= s
    return x


def record_candidate(pool, arr, score, max_pool=10):
    """Keep a small rolling pool of candidate arrays."""
    if arr is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return pool
    if len(pool) >= max_pool:
        pool = pool[1:]
    pool.append(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    return pool


def fft_convolve_full(a):
    """Full linear convolution of a with itself, via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 1
    L = pow2_ge(2 * n - 1)
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, L


def fft_score(a):
    """Score and convolution coefficients using FFT normalization."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    c, _ = fft_convolve_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf, c
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * a.size * cmax), c


def hankel_cross_with_fft(a, W_fft, Lx):
    """
    For weights w, compute s_i = sum_j a_j w_{i+j}.
    W_fft must be np.fft.rfft(w_padded, n=Lx), with Lx >= 3*n-2.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    ar = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, n=Lx)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * W_fft, n=Lx)
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def make_weights(c, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop):
    """
    Build nonnegative weights from convolution coefficients.
    top_k     : number of top coefficients for the top component.
    soft_frac : scale for soft weights, relative to (max-mean).
    top_mix   : mixture fraction of top-k weights.
    use_prop  : if True, use weights proportional to c instead of softmax.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    cmean = float(np.mean(c))

    if use_prop:
        eps = max(1e-9, 1e-6 * max(1.0, cmean))
        w_soft = c + eps
        s = float(np.sum(w_soft))
        if s > 0:
            w_soft = w_soft / s
        else:
            w_soft = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        scale = max(float(soft_frac) * max(1e-12, cmax - cmean), 1e-12)
        z = (c - cmax) / scale
        w_soft = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(w_soft))
        if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
            w_soft = w_soft / s
        else:
            w_soft = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)

    if top_k <= 0:
        top_w = w_soft.copy()
    else:
        k = int(min(c.size, max(1, int(top_k))))
        if k >= c.size:
            top_w = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / c.size
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            top_w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            top_w[idx] = 1.0 / k

    if top_mix <= 0:
        w = w_soft
    elif top_mix >= 1:
        w = top_w
    else:
        w = (1.0 - float(top_mix)) * w_soft + float(top_mix) * top_w

    w = w + 1e-12
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s > 0:
        w /= s
    else:
        w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return w


def cg_solve_a(w, a_guess, cg_iters=30, tol=1e-6):
    """
    Solve (I + H_w) x = 1 by conjugate gradients.
    Returns normalized nonnegative x or None on failure.
    """
    a_guess = np.asarray(a_guess, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a_guess.size)
    if n == 0:
        return None

    Lx = pow2_ge(3 * n - 2 if n > 1 else 1)
    W_fft = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64), n=Lx)
    b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    s_guess = float(np.sum(a_guess)) if np.all(np.isfinite(a_guess)) else 0.0
    if s_guess > 0:
        x = a_guess * (float(n) / s_guess)
    else:
        x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    r = b - (x + hankel_cross_with_fft(x, W_fft, Lx))
    p = r.copy()
    rs = float(np.dot(r, r))

    if not np.isfinite(rs) or rs <= 1e-300:
        s = float(np.sum(x))
        if s > 0:
            return x / s
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    for _ in range(int(max(0, cg_iters))):
        Ap = p + hankel_cross_with_fft(p, W_fft, Lx)
        pAp = float(np.dot(p, Ap))
        if not np.isfinite(pAp) or pAp <= 1e-14:
            break
        alpha = rs / pAp
        x = x + alpha * p
        r = r - alpha * Ap
        rs_new = float(np.dot(r, r))
        if not np.isfinite(rs_new):
            break
        if np.sqrt(rs_new) < max(tol, 1e-10):
            break
        beta = rs_new / rs if rs > 0 else 0.0
        p = r + beta * p
        rs = rs_new

    x = np.real(x)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e6)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 0:
        return None
    return x / s


def kkt_search(a, time_limit=30.0, settings=None, cg_iters=30, tol=1e-6, seed=1):
    """
    Reweighted KKT fixed-point local search.
    settings is a list of tuples:
        (top_k_ratio, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop)
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if settings is None:
        settings = [
            (0.08, 0.8, 0.0, False),
            (0.03, 0.15, 0.4, False),
            (0.01, 0.05, 1.0, False),
            (0.10, 0.35, 0.2, True),
        ]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))

    best = a.copy()
    best_score, _ = fft_score(a)
    last_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        score, c = fft_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            last_improve = it

        if best_score <= 1.01:
            break

        # If we have wandered far from the current best, pull back.
        if np.isfinite(best_score) and score > 1.05 * best_score + 1e-6:
            a = normalize_nonneg(0.7 * a + 0.3 * best)

        # Random kick if stuck.
        if it - last_improve > 80:
            a = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.06, a.size)))
            last_improve = it

        st = settings[it % len(settings)]
        top_ratio, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop = st
        top_k = max(4, min(c.size, int(round(float(a.size) * float(top_ratio)))))
        w = make_weights(c, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop)

        x = cg_solve_a(w, a, cg_iters=cg_iters, tol=tol)
        if x is None:
            # Fallback: small multiplicative noise.
            a = normalize_nonneg(a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, a.size)))
            it += 1
            continue

        if it < 10:
            mixf = 0.45
        elif it < 40:
            mixf = 0.75
        elif it < 100:
            mixf = 0.92
        else:
            mixf = 1.0

        a_new = (1.0 - mixf) * a + mixf * x
        if it % 250 == 0:
            a_new = 0.999 * a_new + 0.001 * np.ones_like(a_new)
        a = normalize_nonneg(a_new)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            break
        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def gradient_refine(a, time_limit=30.0, settings=None, lr=0.30, seed=1):
    """Multiplicative gradient refinement using top/soft/proportional weights."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if settings is None:
        settings = [
            (0.05, 0.25, 0.3, False),
            (0.02, 0.10, 0.6, False),
            (0.10, 0.50, 0.2, True),
        ]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    best = a.copy()
    best_score, _ = fft_score(a)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        score, c = fft_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
        if best_score <= 1.01:
            break

        st = settings[it % len(settings)]
        top_ratio, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop = st
        top_k = max(4, min(c.size, int(round(float(a.size) * float(top_ratio)))))
        w = make_weights(c, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop)

        Lx = pow2_ge(3 * a.size - 2)
        W_fft = np.fft.rfft(w, n=Lx)
        g = a + hankel_cross_with_fft(a, W_fft, Lx)

        # Remove the irrelevant mean offset and scale to a stable range.
        g = g - float(np.median(g))
        scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
        if scale > 1e-14:
            g = np.tanh(g / scale)
        else:
            g = g - g

        step = float(lr) * np.exp(-float(it) / 2500.0)
        a = a * np.exp(-step * g)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)

        # Occasionally add a tiny positive floor to escape exact zeros.
        if it % 400 == 0:
            a = 0.9995 * a + 0.0005 * np.ones_like(a)

        a = normalize_nonneg(a)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            a = normalize_nonneg(best)
        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def block_search(a, time_limit=30.0, seed=1):
    """Simple randomized block multiplicative hill climbing."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))

    best = a.copy()
    best_score, _ = fft_score(a)
    sigma = 0.12
    fails = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = best.copy()

        width_min = max(1, n // 500)
        width_max = max(2, n // 25)
        width = int(rng.integers(width_min, width_max + 1))
        if width >= n:
            width = max(1, n // 10)

        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        cand[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, width))

        if rng.random() < 0.05:
            cand = 0.7 * cand + 0.3 * cand[::-1]

        cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
        score, _ = fft_score(cand)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = cand
            sigma = min(0.18, sigma * 1.15)
            fails = 0
        else:
            fails += 1
            sigma = max(0.03, sigma * 0.97)
            if fails > 60:
                cand = best.copy()
                idx = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                cand[idx] = max(1e-9, cand[idx] * float(rng.uniform(0.2, 5.0)))
                best = normalize_nonneg(best)
                fails = 0

    return best, best_score


def make_basis(n, D):
    """Chebyshev basis T_1,...,T_D on midpoints of [0,1]."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    D = int(max(1, D))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    B = np.empty((n, D), dtype=np.float64)

    T0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    T1 = t.copy()
    B[:, 0] = T1
    if D >= 2:
        for k in range(2, D + 1):
            Tk = 2.0 * t * T1 - T0
            B[:, k - 1] = Tk
            T0, T1 = T1, Tk
    return B


def sequence_from_basis(q, B):
    """q -> nonnegative normalized density on the grid."""
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if B.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
    z = B.dot(q)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(B.shape[0], dtype=np.float64) / B.shape[0]
    return e / s


def random_q(D, seed, amp=0.7):
    """Random low-degree Chebyshev log-density coefficients."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    q = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=int(D))
    decay = np.exp(-0.15 * np.arange(int(D)))
    q *= decay * float(amp) * float(0.5 + rng.random())
    return q


def param_search(n, D, time_limit=30.0, q0=None, settings=None, lr=0.006, seed=1):
    """
    Adam-like optimization of a smooth Chebyshev-parameterized density.
    Returns (best_array, best_score, best_q).
    """
    if settings is None:
        settings = [
            (0.05, 0.25, 0.3, False),
            (0.02, 0.10, 0.6, False),
            (0.10, 0.50, 0.2, True),
        ]

    B = make_basis(n, D)
    if q0 is None:
        q = random_q(D, seed, amp=0.8)
    else:
        q = np.asarray(q0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if q.size != D:
            tmp = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(D, q.size)
            tmp[:m] = q[:m]
            q = tmp

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))

    m = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)

    best_q = q.copy()
    best_a = sequence_from_basis(best_q, B)
    best_score, _ = fft_score(best_a)

    it = 0
    t = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a = sequence_from_basis(q, B)
        score, c = fft_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_q = q.copy()
        if best_score <= 1.01:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
            q = random_q(D, seed + it, amp=0.7)

        st = settings[it % len(settings)]
        top_ratio, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop = st
        top_k = max(4, min(c.size, int(round(float(a.size) * float(top_ratio)))))
        w = make_weights(c, top_k, soft_frac, top_mix, use_prop)

        Lx = pow2_ge(3 * int(a.size) - 2)
        W_fft = np.fft.rfft(w, n=Lx)
        g = a + hankel_cross_with_fft(a, W_fft, Lx)

        # Chain rule through softmax(q): grad_q = a * (gz - dot(a,gz)).
        dot_val = float(np.dot(a, g))
        grad_a = a * (g - dot_val)
        grad = B.T.dot(grad_a)

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm > 1e-14 and np.isfinite(gnorm):
            grad = grad / gnorm
        else:
            grad = rng.normal(0.0, 1e-4, size=D)

        t += 1
        b1 = 0.9
        b2 = 0.999
        eps = 1e-8
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)
        mh = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vh = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        q = q - float(lr) * mh / np.sqrt(vh + eps)
        q = np.clip(q, -20.0, 20.0)

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score, best_q


def gaussian_start(n, mu, sigma):
    """Gaussian bump sequence."""
    x = (np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    sigma = max(float(sigma), 1e-6)
    return np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - float(mu)) / sigma) ** 2)


def make_start_collection(n, seed):
    """Generate diverse initial sequences of length n."""
    n = int(max(16, n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    raw = []
    raw.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    raw.append(x.copy())
    raw.append((1.0 - x).copy())

    for p in [0.5, 1.5, 2.0]:
        raw.append(np.power(np.maximum(x, 1e-8), p))
        raw.append(np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-8), p))

    for s in [1.0, 2.5, 5.0, 10.0]:
        raw.append(np.exp(s * (x - 0.5)))
        raw.append(np.exp(-s * (x - 0.5)))

    raw.append(gaussian_start(n, 0.25, 0.08))
    raw.append(gaussian_start(n, 0.25, 0.20))
    raw.append(gaussian_start(n, 0.75, 0.08))
    raw.append(gaussian_start(n, 0.75, 0.20))
    raw.append(gaussian_start(n, 0.50, 0.12))
    raw.append(gaussian_start(n, 0.50, 0.30))

    g1 = gaussian_start(n, 0.30, 0.12)
    g2 = gaussian_start(n, 0.70, 0.12)
    raw.append(0.4 * g1 + 0.6 * g2)
    raw.append(0.6 * g1 + 0.4 * g2)

    # Low-frequency random periodic log-density shapes.
    for _ in range(3):
        M = 4
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.5))
        coeffs = amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=M)
        phas = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=M)
        vals = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(M):
            vals += coeffs[m] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (m + 1) * (x - 0.5) + phas[m])
        raw.append(np.exp(vals))

    raw.append(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0, size=n))

    out = []
    for arr in raw:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if arr.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            continue
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s > 0:
            arr /= s
            out.append(arr)
    return out


def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    """Resample a normalized sequence to a new length using midpoint interpolation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n_old = int(a.size)
    n_new = int(max(1, n_new))
    if n_old == n_new:
        return a
    if n_old <= 1:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / n_new

    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n_new)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(y)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Search under the wall-clock budget and return the best sequence found."""
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(1.0, budget_s))
    deadline = start_time + max(0.6, budget - 25.0)

    # Parent sequence, if available.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
            parent = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)

    if parent.size > 8192:
        parent = resample_sequence(parent, 4096)
    parent = normalize_nonneg(parent)

    best_arr = parent.copy()
    best_score, _ = fft_score(parent)
    pool = [parent.copy()]

    kkt_settings = [
        (0.08, 0.80, 0.0, False),
        (0.03, 0.15, 0.4, False),
        (0.01, 0.05, 1.0, False),
        (0.10, 0.35, 0.2, True),
    ]
    grad_settings = [
        (0.05, 0.25, 0.3, False),
        (0.02, 0.10, 0.6, False),
        (0.10, 0.50, 0.2, True),
    ]

    def current_rem():
        return deadline - time.monotonic()

    # 1) Improve the provided parent if it is not too large.
    if best_arr.size <= 8192:
        rem = current_rem()
        lim = min(120.0, rem - 2.0)
        if lim > 10.0:
            print("phase: parent KKT search, len={} time={:.1f}".format(int(best_arr.size), lim))
            cand, sc = kkt_search(
                best_arr, lim,
                settings=kkt_settings,
                cg_iters=35,
                tol=1e-6,
                seed=int(seed) + 1
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = cand.copy()
                pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
                print("new best from parent KKT: {:.9f}".format(best_score))

    # 2) Main mid-length search.
    n_main = 2048
    starts_main = make_start_collection(n_main, int(seed) + 10)
    for i, st in enumerate(starts_main[:8]):
        rem = current_rem()
        lim = min(38.0, rem - 4.0)
        if lim < 8.0:
            break
        cand, sc = kkt_search(
            st, lim,
            settings=kkt_settings,
            cg_iters=28,
            tol=1e-6,
            seed=int(seed) + 100 + i
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = cand.copy()
            pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
            print("new best from mid KKT #{}: {:.9f}".format(i, best_score))

    # 3) A few higher-resolution tries.
    n_high = 4096
    starts_high = make_start_collection(n_high, int(seed) + 20)[:3]
    for i, st in enumerate(starts_high):
        rem = current_rem()
        lim = min(45.0, rem - 4.0)
        if lim < 8.0:
            break
        cand, sc = kkt_search(
            st, lim,
            settings=kkt_settings,
            cg_iters=22,
            tol=1e-6,
            seed=int(seed) + 200 + i
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = cand.copy()
            pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
            print("new best from high KKT #{}: {:.9f}".format(i, best_score))

    # 4) Smooth Chebyshev parameterization.
    D = 10
    param_best_score = np.inf
    param_best_info = None
    q_starts = [
        random_q(D, int(seed) + 300, amp=0.5),
        random_q(D, int(seed) + 301, amp=1.1),
    ]
    q_slope_plus = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    q_slope_plus[0] = 2.5
    q_starts.append(q_slope_plus)
    q_slope_minus = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    q_slope_minus[0] = -3.0
    q_starts.append(q_slope_minus)

    for i, q0 in enumerate(q_starts):
        rem = current_rem()
        lim = min(40.0, rem - 4.0)
        if lim < 6.0:
            break
        cand, sc, qbest = param_search(
            2048, D, lim, q0=q0,
            settings=kkt_settings,
            lr=0.006,
            seed=int(seed) + 400 + i
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = cand.copy()
            pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
            print("new best from param #{}: {:.9f}".format(i, best_score))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < param_best_score:
            param_best_score = sc
            param_best_info = (qbest, D, 2048)

    # 5) If still above target, upscale current best and refine.
    if best_arr is not None and best_score > 1.5027 and best_arr.size < 8192:
        rem = current_rem()
        if rem > 80.0:
            n_up = min(8192, max(4096, 2 * int(best_arr.size)))
            a_up = resample_sequence(best_arr, n_up)

            lim = min(120.0, rem / 2.0 - 4.0)
            if lim > 10.0:
                cand, sc = kkt_search(
                    a_up, lim,
                    settings=kkt_settings,
                    cg_iters=24,
                    tol=1e-6,
                    seed=int(seed) + 500
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_arr = cand.copy()
                    pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
                    print("new best after upsample KKT: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
                else:
                    cand = a_up

            rem = current_rem()
            lim = min(60.0, rem - 4.0)
            if lim > 8.0:
                cand2, sc = gradient_refine(
                    cand, lim,
                    settings=grad_settings,
                    lr=0.30,
                    seed=int(seed) + 501
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_arr = cand2.copy()
                    pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
                    print("new best after upsample gradient: {:.9f}".format(best_score))

    # 6) If a good smooth Chebyshev model was found, lift it to higher resolution.
    if param_best_info is not None and param_best_score < 1.62:
        rem = current_rem()
        if rem > 60.0:
            qbest, Dbest, nbest = param_best_info
            n_up = min(4096, max(nbest, best_arr.size))
            B = make_basis(n_up, Dbest)
            a_up = sequence_from_basis(qbest, B)
            lim = min(60.0, rem / 2.0 - 4.0)
            if lim > 8.0:
                cand, sc = kkt_search(
                    a_up, lim,
                    settings=kkt_settings,
                    cg_iters=24,
                    tol=1e-6,
                    seed=int(seed) + 600
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_arr = cand.copy()
                    pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
                    print("new best from lifted param model: {:.9f}".format(best_score))

    # 7) Final gradient refinement of the current best.
    rem = current_rem()
    if best_arr is not None and rem > 60.0:
        lim = min(150.0, 0.45 * rem)
        cand, sc = gradient_refine(
            best_arr, lim,
            settings=grad_settings,
            lr=0.28,
            seed=int(seed) + 700
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = cand.copy()
            pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
            print("new best after final gradient: {:.9f}".format(best_score))

    # 8) Final randomized block hill climb.
    rem = current_rem()
    if best_arr is not None and rem > 35.0:
        lim = min(90.0, rem - 10.0)
        cand, sc = block_search(
            best_arr, lim,
            seed=int(seed) + 800
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = cand.copy()
            pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
            print("new best after block search: {:.9f}".format(best_score))

    # If we are close, do one conservative exact-evaluation-guided squeeze.
    rem = current_rem()
    if best_arr is not None and best_score <= 1.60 and rem > 35.0:
        try:
            seq_check = [float(x) for x in best_arr]
            exact_check = float(evaluate_sequence(seq_check))
            if not np.isfinite(exact_check) or exact_check > 1.5027:
                lim = min(45.0, rem / 2.0 - 5.0)
                if lim > 6.0:
                    cand, sc = gradient_refine(
                        best_arr, lim,
                        settings=grad_settings,
                        lr=0.22,
                        seed=int(seed) + 900
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_arr = cand.copy()
                        pool = record_candidate(pool, best_arr, best_score, 12)
                        print("new best after exact-guided squeeze: {:.9f}".format(best_score))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Choose the best by exact evaluation among a few final candidates.
    final_candidates = list(pool[-6:])
    if best_arr is not None:
        final_candidates.append(best_arr)

    final_arr = best_arr
    final_score = best_score
    for arr in final_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        if arr is None:
            continue
        try:
            seq_try = [float(x) for x in arr]
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(seq_try))
        except Exception:
            ex = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < final_score:
            final_score = ex
            final_arr = arr

    final_arr = np.asarray(final_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if final_arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(final_arr)):
        final_arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final_arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        final_arr += 0.05
        final_arr /= final_arr.sum()
        from functools import reduce
        # Avoid any weird zero-sum artifacts.
    else:
        final_arr /= s

    print("final sequence length: {}".format(int(final_arr.size)))
    print("best fft score: {:.9f}".format(float(best_score)))
    print("final exact score: {:.9f}".format(float(final_score)))

    return [float(x) for x in final_arr]
```
